{"session": "01a0f746-bf0e-7cb3-80cd-9c63c2d7bef2", "timestamp": "2026-10-01T11:55:46.589Z", "call": {"type": "custom_tool_call", "id": "ctc_07f65a16992a408d016abe4a40871887d29abd30243d623ae9", "status": "completed", "call_id": "call_yIR4sivTMda9GnDhqzoV40P7", "name": "exec", "input": "text(await tools.exec_command({cmd:\"cat /private/var/folders/cs/vgp_x_353zd9frkjpysp7zdw0000gn/T/kntnt-native-session-zhagrwr9/scratch/write-source-check-6lwyn1b1/report-1.md\",max_output_tokens:22000}));\n", "internal_chat_message_metadata_passthrough": {"turn_id": "01a0f746-c0d6-7bd0-8143-630450bece1f", "create_time": 1790855742.445305}}}
{"session": "01a0f746-bf0e-7cb3-80cd-9c63c2d7bef2", "timestamp": "2026-10-01T11:55:46.716Z", "result": {"type": "custom_tool_call_output", "id": "ctco_01a0f752-149b-77f2-a758-7ce2fa39c40f", "call_id": "call_yIR4sivTMda9GnDhqzoV40P7", "output": [{"type": "input_text", "text": "Script completed\nWall time 0.1 seconds\nOutput:\n"}, {"type": "input_text", "text": "{\"chunk_id\":\"32db0b\",\"wall_time_seconds\":0.000002208,\"exit_code\":0,\"original_token_count\":6986,\"output\":\"# Source comparison 1\\n\\n## Scope and method\\n\\nI read the complete draft and the complete supplied Swedish brief, source material and boundaries. This report compares claims, attribution, qualifications and implications. It does not review prose quality or mechanics. The material is synthetic and fictional; no external evidence was used. The target language is British English. There are no speech quotations in the draft, so there are no translated-quotation findings. Changes of meaning in unquoted translation are treated as source-support findings.\\n\\nThe commissioning brief establishes the proposed position: “Avsändare är Sanna Ek, talesperson för föreningen Öppna beslut. Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.” Advocacy and evaluative reasoning expressed in this commissioned voice are distinguished below from assertions about what the documents establish.\\n\\n## Complete claim accounting\\n\\nEach row quotes the source in Swedish. Where a sentence contains several claims, the row identifies and accounts for each. Pronoun features are accounted for separately below.\\n\\n| Draft passage | Source passage in its own words and language | Comparison |\\n| --- | --- | --- |\\n| Title: “Lervik needs evidence before ending telephone bookings” | “Skriv en debattartikel för invånarna i fiktiva Lervik.”; “Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.”; “Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna.” | The place is the fictional town in the brief. The need for evidence is the author's advocated conclusion from the missing measurements and the requested postponement. The title does not assert that a termination has already occurred. Supported as advocacy. |\\n| “The case for removing telephone bookings from Lervik’s association premises rests on avoiding duplicate administration.” | “Tjänsteutlåtandet inför kommunstyrelsens möte den 18 juni föreslår att telefonbokning tas bort för alla sju lokaler från september. Motivet i utlåtandet är att personalen ska slippa föra in uppgifter i två flöden.”; “Förvaltningens verkliga invändning mot att behålla båda kanalerna är dubbel administration, inte att telefonanvändare är lata eller dyra.” | Same proposal and administrative motive; “rests on” attributes the case to the supplied motive, without adding a demonstrated benefit. “Lervik’s association premises” identifies the municipal debate’s premises. No finding. |\\n| “But the papers provide no measurement of staff time or calculated savings.” | “Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna.” | The explicit absence in the papers is preserved. “Staff time” is the contextual time expenditure of the booking administration. The source specifies financial savings; “calculated savings” in this introduction is less explicit, but the body expressly supplies “financial savings”, and the ordinary contextual reading is monetary savings. No defensible source-support finding in the complete draft. |\\n| “The municipal executive board should establish what each route costs and why people use it.” | “Under försöket ska förvaltningen registrera tidsåtgång per bokningsväg och be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb.”; “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.” | An advocated evidential requirement, based on the planned time recording, voluntary reasons and cost comparison. It does not assert that the association already knows either route’s cost. The introduction does not describe the collection method; the body preserves voluntariness. No finding. |\\n| “By Sanna Ek, spokesperson for Öppna beslut” | “Avsändare är Sanna Ek, talesperson för föreningen Öppna beslut.” | Same named person, role and represented association. No finding. |\\n| “A staff report for the 18 June meeting of Lervik’s municipal executive board proposes ending telephone booking at all seven premises for local associations from September.” | “Tjänsteutlåtandet inför kommunstyrelsens möte den 18 juni föreslår att telefonbokning tas bort för alla sju lokaler från september.”; the preceding paragraph identifies the subject as “föreningslokaler”. | “Staff report” appropriately names the administrative statement. Meeting date, proposal modality, seven-premises scope and September start are preserved. No year is added to the June/September references. “For local associations” adds an eligibility/purpose qualification not supplied by “föreningslokaler”: finding F2. |\\n| “Öppna beslut wants that permanent switch postponed while both telephone and web booking are tested for six months.” | “Avsändare är Sanna Ek, talesperson för föreningen Öppna beslut. Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.”; “Öppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar.” | The association’s commissioned position and explicit proposal support this attribution. Permanent versus temporary scope, postponement, both channels and six-month duration are preserved. The next proposal paragraph gives all-seven scope. No finding. |\\n| “I do not oppose digital booking” | “Hon motsätter sig inte digital bokning.” | The bylined speaker is Sanna Ek, so third-person source attribution becomes her first-person statement. Same position and certainty. No finding. |\\n| “I question whether the evidence justifies removing the alternative.” | “Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.”; “Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna.”; “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.” | Commissioned argument and reflection, not a new event or purported quotation. It preserves support for digital booking while challenging removal before the advocated comparison. No finding. |\\n| Heading: “The pilot leaves users’ reasons unanswered” | The pilot’s actual stated omissions are: “Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana.” The future proposal is: “Under försöket ska förvaltningen registrera tidsåtgång per bokningsväg och be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb.” | Neither passage states that the pilot collected no reasons or that its report leaves reasons unanswered. A proposed later inquiry does not establish that no earlier inquiry answered them. Finding F1. |\\n| “The municipality’s pilot report, Booking Association Premises, dated 8 April 2026, records 96 web bookings and 24 telephone bookings during an eight-week trial at two venues.” | “Kommunens pilotrapport Bokning av föreningslokaler, 8 april 2026, gäller ett åtta veckor långt försök i två lokaler. Under försöket gjordes 96 bokningar via webb och 24 via telefon.” | Institution, report identity/title, date, eight-week period, two-premises scope, both figures and channel allocation match. “Records” attributes the figures to the named report as required. “Venues” here refers back to association premises; it does not introduce an outdoor location or another kind of place. No finding. |\\n| “Those figures describe bookings, not unique individuals.” | “Rapporten räknar bokningar, inte unika personer.” | The counted unit is correctly preserved. The draft describes the two figures instead of making a broader statement about everything in the report; this makes no contrary assertion about other possible report contents. No finding. |\\n| “It did not measure age, functional ability or familiarity with digital technology.” | “Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana.” | Same three expressly unmeasured characteristics. “Functional ability” renders “funktionsförmåga”; “familiarity with digital technology” expresses the digital familiarity/experience at issue, without turning it into demonstrated ability or incapacity. The past tense situates the completed report/trial and adds no changed observation. No finding. |\\n| “We cannot use the telephone bookings to calculate how many residents cannot book online.” | “Telefonbokningarna kan därför inte användas för att säga hur stor andel av invånarna som inte kan boka digitalt.” | The inference limit and residents as the population are preserved. “How many” expresses population magnitude rather than explicitly saying “proportion”; the source’s inability to infer the proportion also prevents deriving an absolute number from those bookings. A known population denominator would connect the two forms, but does not supply the missing inability information. “Online” refers to the contrasted web channel, not to some additional channel. No finding. |\\n| “A booking made by telephone proves no inability to use the web” | “Sanna Eks ståndpunkt: ‘Att en bokning går via telefon är inte ett bevis för att någon inte kan använda webben.’” | Same position: absence of evidential implication from the booking channel to the user’s inability. It does not assert that the user is able to use the web, nor that nobody is unable. No finding. |\\n| “it gives us a reason to ask why that route is still used.” | “Det är ett skäl att ta reda på varför telefonen fortfarande används.” | Same advocated reason for inquiry and continued telephone use. “Us” is rhetorical inclusion, not an assertion of a measured group or its actual experiences. No finding. |\\n| Heading: “Duplicate administration deserves a fair assessment” | “Förvaltningens verkliga invändning mot att behålla båda kanalerna är dubbel administration, inte att telefonanvändare är lata eller dyra.”; “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.” | The administrative objection is factual and supported; deserving a fair assessment is authorial valuation consistent with weighing it. No finding. |\\n| “The administration wants staff to avoid entering information through two workflows.” | “Motivet i utlåtandet är att personalen ska slippa föra in uppgifter i två flöden.” | Same administrative intention, staff subject and two information-entry flows. No claim of an achieved saving or causal effect is added. No finding. |\\n| “That objection deserves to be taken seriously.” | “Förvaltningens verkliga invändning mot att behålla båda kanalerna är dubbel administration”; “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.” | An authorial valuation of the supported objection, not a new attributed external assessment. No finding. |\\n| “Its case concerns duplicate administration, not a claim that telephone users are lazy or costly.” | “Förvaltningens verkliga invändning mot att behålla båda kanalerna är dubbel administration, inte att telefonanvändare är lata eller dyra.” | The true objection and expressly excluded alternative characterisations match. “Costly” renders the attributed characterisation “dyra”; this does not state that telephone users have zero administrative cost. No finding. |\\n| “Yet the papers contain neither a measurement of staff time nor a calculation of financial savings.” | “Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna.” | Same explicit absence, with the financial qualification preserved. No finding. |\\n| “Before making a permanent change, the board should be able to weigh the work involved against the value for users.” | “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.”; “Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler”. | The board is the relevant municipal decision maker; priority before change and user value are supported. The draft’s “work involved” replaces the cost of labour with the work/effort itself. Those are not interchangeable in this cost-comparison context. Finding F3. |\\n| “The pilot’s booking totals cannot settle that judgement.” | “Rapporten räknar bokningar, inte unika personer. Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana.”; “Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna.”; “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.” | Authorial reasoning: booking counts alone do not supply the missing labour-cost and user-value comparison. No claim that the report lacks every other conceivable datum is made in this sentence. No finding. |\\n| Heading: “A wider trial can inform the decision” | “Kommunens pilotrapport ... gäller ett åtta veckor långt försök i två lokaler.”; “Öppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler”; “Därefter kan den avgöra om en kanal ska tas bort, ändras eller behållas.” | Wider venue coverage is supported by two versus seven. “Can inform” is a proportionate authorial expectation, not a demonstrated technical or causal effect. No finding. |\\n| “Our proposal covers all seven venues for six months, keeping both booking routes.” | “Öppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar.” | Same association proposal, all-seven scope, six-month duration and retention of both routes. “Our” is the spokesperson’s association voice. No finding. |\\n| “Staff would record the time spent on each route and ask users to explain their choice voluntarily.” | “Under försöket ska förvaltningen registrera tidsåtgång per bokningsväg och be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb.” | This is the proposed trial procedure, so the conditional does not weaken an implemented commitment. “Staff” names the administration’s personnel executing the attributed administrative activity in ordinary institutional usage; it adds no specified individual, headcount or manual mechanism. The channel-by-channel time scope, request for reasons and voluntary user response are preserved. No finding. |\\n| “This would help the board assess both the workload and users’ reasons.” | “Under försöket ska förvaltningen registrera tidsåtgång per bokningsväg och be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb.”; “Därefter kan den avgöra om en kanal ska tas bort, ändras eller behållas.” | An authorial inference about information from the proposed observations. This refers to assessing workload, which the time recording supports; unlike F3, it does not translate the specific labour-cost comparison as workload. No observed causal effect is claimed. No finding. |\\n| Heading: “The board should postpone the permanent switch” | “Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.” | Exact commissioned advocacy, preserving its proposed rather than achieved status. No finding. |\\n| “Öppna beslut makes no claim to have funded or costed the trial.” | “Föreningen gör inget anspråk på att ha finansierat eller kostnadsberäknat försöket.” | Same association, completed-action scope, funding and cost calculation, and absence of an assertion. The draft correctly does not turn this into a factual assertion that no funding or costing exists. No finding. |\\n| “The executive board must consider its cost, postpone the permanent switch and first test both routes.” | “Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till.”; “Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.” | This is the concluding advocated course of action. “Consider” is less precise than the source’s requirement to take a position on the cost, but the sentence is authorial advocacy and does not assert that a financial decision is already made. It retains the unresolved cost and the requested sequence. I find no source-support defect warranting a repair to this sentence. |\\n| “It can then decide whether a booking channel should be removed, changed or retained.” | “Därefter kan den avgöra om en kanal ska tas bort, ändras eller behållas.” | Same board, subsequent decision, modal possibility and all three named outcomes. “Then” follows the preceding first test, preserving the temporal dependency. No finding. |\\n\\n## Pronouns: referents and independently conveyed features\\n\\nThe draft supplies no natural-gender pronoun. The source supplies natural-gender information about Sanna through “Hon”, but the draft’s “I” and byline do not themselves assert that feature. The source’s “den” and the draft’s institutional “it” convey grammatical agreement/reference, not human natural gender.\\n\\n| Draft occurrence | Referent and source support | Person, number and natural-gender accounting |\\n| --- | --- | --- |\\n| “I do not oppose”; “I question” | Sanna Ek, established by “Avsändare är Sanna Ek, talesperson för föreningen Öppna beslut” and “Hon motsätter sig inte digital bokning.” | “I” is first-person singular. The source gives one named speaker, and the commissioned first-person voice supports that change of person. English “I” conveys no natural gender. The source “Hon” conveys female natural gender, but that information is not newly asserted by the draft pronoun. |\\n| “It did not measure” | The pilot report: “Kommunens pilotrapport ... Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana.” | Third-person singular inanimate reference. Swedish “Den” is common grammatical gender for “rapport”; it does not convey natural gender. English “It” adds none. |\\n| “We cannot use” | Rhetorical speaker-and-reader inclusion for the inference limit: “Telefonbokningarna kan därför inte användas ...”. | First-person plural. No exact membership, participant count or measured behaviour is asserted. It expresses the same general limit on any inference-maker, rather than inventing a plural research group. No natural gender. |\\n| “it gives us a reason” | “It” refers to a telephone booking/the fact of that booking; “us” includes speaker and audience. Source: “Att en bokning går via telefon ... Det är ett skäl att ta reda på varför telefonen fortfarande används.” | “It”: third-person singular, no natural gender. “Us”: first-person plural rhetorical inclusion, with no factual assertion about a group’s composition. The source “Det” is grammatical neuter reference to the proposition, not natural gender. |\\n| “Its case concerns” | The administration: “Förvaltningens verkliga invändning ...”. | Third-person singular possessive institution, no natural gender. Swedish “förvaltning” is common grammatical gender; an institution is not thereby a female or male person. |\\n| “Our proposal” | Öppna beslut’s proposal, voiced by its spokesperson: “Öppna beslut föreslår ...”. | First-person plural possessive collective voice. The brief establishes the association represented by Sanna. The pronoun gives no exact count, individual members or natural genders. |\\n| “their choice” | The users: “be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb.” | Third-person plural possessive. Swedish “användarna” and “de” supply plural users; English “their” likewise conveys plural context, with no natural-gender assertion. |\\n| “This would help” | The just-described time recording and voluntary collection of reasons: “registrera tidsåtgång per bokningsväg och be användarna frivilligt ange varför ...”. | Demonstrative singular reference to a procedure. No human person or natural gender is conveyed. |\\n| “consider its cost” | The proposed trial: “Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till”; “försöket”. | Third-person singular possessive, inanimate. The source’s “försöket” has grammatical neuter gender; neither expression conveys natural gender. |\\n| “It can then decide” | The executive board: “Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till. Därefter kan den avgöra ...”. | Third-person singular institution. Swedish “den” agrees with common-gender “kommunstyrelsen”; it supplies no natural gender. English “It” does not invent one. |\\n\\n“That” in “that permanent switch”, “that route”, “That objection” and “that judgement” is demonstrative reference to an already introduced switch, telephone route, administrative objection and comparison. “Those” in “Those figures” is plural demonstrative reference to the two stated booking figures. These expressions add no person, natural gender, or new measured population. “Each route”, “both routes” and “both booking routes” match the two source routes, “telefon eller webb” / “båda bokningsvägarna”.\\n\\n## Source-support findings\\n\\n### F1: The heading turns an unreported matter into a pilot finding\\n\\n**Draft:** “The pilot leaves users’ reasons unanswered”.\\n\\n**Source:** “Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana.” The proposed later procedure is “be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb”. Neither states that the earlier report has no explanation of users’ reasons. Sanna’s supported position is “Det är ett skäl att ta reda på varför telefonen fortfarande används.” That position supports an inquiry, not a factual description of all the pilot’s contents.\\n\\n**Difference:** Scope and state of knowledge. The source specifies three measurements not made and says why further inquiry is advocated. The heading asserts the outcome/content of the pilot in relation to users’ reasons.\\n\\n**Concrete case:** The pilot could have recorded telephone users’ stated reasons in an open comments field, while measuring none of age, functional ability or digital familiarity. Its report could explain those reasons, and the association could still advocate collecting reasons in a wider trial. Every supplied statement would remain true, but the heading would fail. No supplied passage excludes that case.\\n\\n**Reverse-direction check:** A pilot without any reasons data but with measured ages would leave reasons unanswered while violating the source’s actual omission statement. This case is excluded by “Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana.” It therefore provides no independent reverse finding. There is no supplied source assertion about reasons that is equivalent to the heading.\\n\\n**Smallest supported repair:** “The pilot figures leave users’ reasons unanswered”. This confines the claim to the supplied booking totals, which identify channels and counts rather than reasons. The report could contain further information without making that repaired heading false.\\n\\n### F2: “Local” adds an unsupported qualification to the associations\\n\\n**Draft:** “all seven premises for local associations”.\\n\\n**Source:** “telefonbokning tas bort för alla sju lokaler från september”, in a source describing “föreningslokaler”.\\n\\n**Difference:** The draft adds the geographical/organisational qualification “local” to the associations served by the premises. The source identifies association premises but does not identify the associations as local or state their eligibility.\\n\\n**Concrete case, source holds and draft fails:** The seven premises could be available to national membership associations or visiting national federations while local associations are not eligible to book them. They would still be “föreningslokaler”, and all the dates, counts, pilot procedure and proposed board action would remain as supplied. They would not be premises for local associations. No supplied statement excludes that case; addressing the town’s residents does not establish who may book its premises.\\n\\n**Reverse-direction check:** Premises for local associations are also association premises in this context. I can name no compatible case in which the draft’s association-purpose description holds but the source’s generic association-purpose description fails. The unsupported narrowing is the finding.\\n\\n**Smallest supported repair:** Replace “all seven premises for local associations” with “all seven association premises”.\\n\\n### F3: The comparison changes labour cost into work/effort\\n\\n**Draft:** “the board should be able to weigh the work involved against the value for users”.\\n\\n**Source:** “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.”\\n\\n**Difference:** The thing to be assessed is changed. “Arbetskostnaden” names the cost of labour; “the work involved” names the work, effort or burden itself. In a source that separately names financial savings and an unresolved trial cost, these are not interchangeable. The user-value side of the comparison and its priority are correctly preserved.\\n\\n**Concrete case, source requirement satisfied but draft requirement not necessarily satisfied:** The board could compare calculated paid labour cost for the two routes with user value without assessing how demanding or onerous staff find the work. It would have the cost comparison Sanna requires but not necessarily an assessment of “the work involved” in the draft’s broader effort sense. A comparison can price task time without assessing the qualitative work burden. Nothing in the source excludes this distinction.\\n\\n**Concrete case, draft requirement satisfied but source requirement not satisfied:** The board could assess the additional tasks, effort and time alongside users’ convenience, while leaving wages, staffing costs and the monetary cost of that labour unknown. That would permit weighing “the work involved”, but would not establish “arbetskostnaden”. It is compatible with Sanna having the supplied stance and with the present documents lacking measurements; her stance need not already have been fulfilled. No supplied statement says work burden and paid labour cost are identical.\\n\\n**Smallest supported repair:** Replace “the work involved” with “the labour cost”: “the board should be able to weigh the labour cost against the value for users.”\\n\\n## Translation findings\\n\\nNone for quoted speech: the draft contains no speech quotation. F3 is a source-support finding arising from changed meaning in unquoted translation. F1 and F2 are source-support findings about added factual implications/qualification. The translated report title, institutional terminology and other paraphrases accounted for above have no identified concrete obstruction to their supported reading.\\n\\n## Completion status\\n\\nComplete: the full draft, including title, deck, byline, headings, body, implications and pronoun features, has been compared with the full supplied material. No source or draft was changed. Unresolved findings: F1, F2 and F3 remain for the parent to adjudicate and repair. No other source-support or translated-quotation findings identified.\\n\"}"}], "internal_chat_message_metadata_passthrough": {"turn_id": "01a0f746-c0d6-7bd0-8143-630450bece1f", "create_time": 1790855746.715979}}}

{"session": "01a0f746-bf0e-7cb3-80cd-9c63c2d7bef2", "timestamp": "2026-10-01T12:04:51.119Z", "result": {"type": "custom_tool_call_output", "id": "ctco_01a0f75a-632f-7201-b354-37066ae27ba5", "call_id": "call_TbWgtobZLp4drv2NjqcD21rX", "output": [{"type": "input_text", "text": "Script completed\nWall time 0.0 seconds\nOutput:\n"}, {"type": "input_text", "text": "{\"chunk_id\":\"304a11\",\"wall_time_seconds\":0.000002208,\"exit_code\":0,\"original_token_count\":7057,\"output\":\"# Source-comparison report\\n\\nDraft: `/private/var/folders/cs/vgp_x_353zd9frkjpysp7zdw0000gn/T/kntnt-native-session-zhagrwr9/scratch/write-source-check-6lwyn1b1/draft.md`\\n\\nSource: `/private/var/folders/cs/vgp_x_353zd9frkjpysp7zdw0000gn/T/kntnt-native-session-zhagrwr9/work/source.md`\\n\\nInvocation: `/write --genre=opinion --language=en_GB --output=response source.md`. Source language: Swedish. Target language: British English. The supplied material is explicitly synthetic and fictional. This comparison concerns source support, including meaning changed in translation. It does not perform general editorial or mechanical review. The draft contains no speech quotations.\\n\\n## Complete claim accounting\\n\\nEach numbered item pairs a draft passage with the applicable Swedish source passage. Related modifiers and qualifications are quoted together. The findings below explain the two scope changes requiring repair.\\n\\n1. **Title: “Lervik needs evidence before ending telephone bookings”.** Source: “Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.” Also: “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.” This is the commissioned author’s advocacy. It does not report a decision already taken or claim that all evidence is absent. Supported.\\n\\n2. **“The case for removing telephone bookings from Lervik’s association premises rests on avoiding duplicate administration.”** Source: “Tjänsteutlåtandet inför kommunstyrelsens möte den 18 juni föreslår att telefonbokning tas bort för alla sju lokaler från september. Motivet i utlåtandet är att personalen ska slippa föra in uppgifter i två flöden.” Also: “Förvaltningens verkliga invändning mot att behålla båda kanalerna är dubbel administration, inte att telefonanvändare är lata eller dyra.” The draft attributes the rationale of the proposal, rather than asserting that the proposal has already saved work. “Association premises” identifies the supplied föreningslokaler; it does not assert ownership by associations. Supported.\\n\\n3. **“But the papers provide no measurement of staff time or calculated savings.”** Source: “Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna.” In context, the time is administrative staff time and the savings are economic savings. The absence is confined to the papers. It does not assert that no one has ever measured time or that no savings could exist. Supported.\\n\\n4. **“The municipal executive board should establish what each route costs and why people use it.”** Source: “Under försöket ska förvaltningen registrera tidsåtgång per bokningsväg och be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb.” Also: “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.” Establishing the cost of each route is the author’s recommendation, resting on the proposed time records and the stated labour-cost comparison. The draft does not assert that such costs are already known. Supported advocacy.\\n\\n5. **“By Sanna Ek, spokesperson for Öppna beslut”.** Source: “Avsändare är Sanna Ek, talesperson för föreningen Öppna beslut.” Identity, role and organisation are supplied. “Spokesperson” adds no sex or gender assertion. Supported.\\n\\n6. **“A staff report for the 18 June meeting of Lervik’s municipal executive board proposes ending telephone booking at all seven association premises from September.”** Source: “Tjänsteutlåtandet inför kommunstyrelsens möte den 18 juni föreslår att telefonbokning tas bort för alla sju lokaler från september.” This preserves the proposal’s status, the meeting date without adding a year, the seven-premises scope and the September start. “Staff report” and “municipal executive board” identify the supplied municipal document and body in this fictional context. No adoption of the proposal is asserted. Supported.\\n\\n7. **“Öppna beslut wants that permanent switch postponed while both telephone and web booking are tested for six months.”** Source: “Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.” Also: “Öppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar.” The draft preserves the organisation’s requested postponement, permanence of the switch, both routes and six-month duration. Supported.\\n\\n8. **“I do not oppose digital booking”.** Source: “Hon motsätter sig inte digital bokning.” The first-person voice is Sanna’s authorised byline voice. The draft does not turn acceptance of digital booking into acceptance of a digital-only policy. Supported.\\n\\n9. **“I question whether the evidence justifies removing the alternative.”** Source: “Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.” Also: “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.” This is the author’s argument about adequacy of the material; it does not attribute a new factual event, measurement or personal experience to Sanna. Supported standpoint.\\n\\n10. **Heading: “The pilot figures leave users’ reasons unanswered”.** Source: “Under försöket gjordes 96 bokningar via webb och 24 via telefon. Rapporten räknar bokningar, inte unika personer.” Also: “Det är ett skäl att ta reda på varför telefonen fortfarande används.” The heading refers to what the figures themselves answer. It does not say that the entire report contains no discussion of reasons. Booking totals do not identify users’ reasons. Supported argument.\\n\\n11. **“The municipality’s pilot report, Booking Association Premises, dated 8 April 2026”.** Source: “Kommunens pilotrapport Bokning av föreningslokaler, 8 april 2026”. The municipality, document type and date are preserved. The title is an English rendering of the Swedish title, without an asserted change to the report’s actual name or publisher. Supported.\\n\\n12. **“records 96 web bookings and 24 telephone bookings during an eight-week trial at two venues.”** Source: “Kommunens pilotrapport Bokning av föreningslokaler, 8 april 2026, gäller ett åtta veckor långt försök i två lokaler. Under försöket gjordes 96 bokningar via webb och 24 via telefon.” The reported amounts retain their routes, the eight-week period and the two-premises scope. The figures are expressly attributed to the pilot report. “Venues” refers back to the specified association premises, so it does not introduce a different measured population. Supported.\\n\\n13. **“Those figures describe bookings, not unique individuals.”** Source: “Rapporten räknar bokningar, inte unika personer.” Same measured unit and same exclusion. “Individuals” here means persons, not households, organisations or accounts. Supported.\\n\\n14. **“It did not measure age, functional ability”.** Source: “Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana.” Report as subject, same absence and same first two variables. Past tense describes the completed pilot report, not a newly asserted historical exclusion. Supported.\\n\\n15. **“or familiarity with digital technology.”** Source: “Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana.” “Digital vana” denotes experience/practice in using digital tools. Familiarity with digital technology can also be recognition or knowledge without that experience. The draft asserts the absence of a different, potentially broader measurement. Source-support finding F1.\\n\\n16. **“We cannot use the telephone bookings to calculate how many residents cannot book online.”** Source: “Telefonbokningarna kan därför inte användas för att säga hur stor andel av invånarna som inte kan boka digitalt.” The source’s precise excluded inference is a proportion of residents unable to book digitally; the draft excludes an absolute count of residents unable to book online. The same adjacent source supplies the booking/person distinction and unmeasured variables, but does not establish equivalence between count and proportion in every compatible case. Source-support finding F2.\\n\\n17. **“A booking made by telephone proves no inability to use the web”.** Source: “Sanna Eks ståndpunkt: ”Att en bokning går via telefon är inte ett bevis för att någon inte kan använda webben. Det är ett skäl att ta reda på varför telefonen fortfarande används.” Detta får citeras eller refereras.” This is an authorised paraphrase of Sanna’s position. In context “proves no inability” means that choosing telephone provides no proof of inability, not that it proves ability. The web is the object in both passages. Supported.\\n\\n18. **“it gives us a reason to ask why that route is still used.”** Source: “Det är ett skäl att ta reda på varför telefonen fortfarande används.” The reason, proposed enquiry and ongoing use of telephone are preserved. The first-person plural invites the organisation/readers into the author’s argument; it does not assert a measured collective view. Supported.\\n\\n19. **Heading: “Duplicate administration deserves a fair assessment”.** Source: “Förvaltningens verkliga invändning mot att behålla båda kanalerna är dubbel administration, inte att telefonanvändare är lata eller dyra.” Also: “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.” Evaluative recommendation about the supplied objection; no claim that an assessment already occurred. Supported authorial argument.\\n\\n20. **“The administration wants staff to avoid entering information through two workflows.”** Source: “Motivet i utlåtandet är att personalen ska slippa föra in uppgifter i två flöden.” Also: “Förvaltningens verkliga invändning mot att behålla båda kanalerna är dubbel administration”. The institution’s stated motive and the work involved are preserved. No extra motive, measured effect or chronology is added. Supported.\\n\\n21. **“That objection deserves to be taken seriously.”** Source: “Förvaltningens verkliga invändning mot att behålla båda kanalerna är dubbel administration”. Also: “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.” This is the author’s own valuation of a supported objection, rather than a factual claim about someone else’s reaction. Supported argument.\\n\\n22. **“Its case concerns duplicate administration, not a claim that telephone users are lazy or costly.”** Source: “Förvaltningens verkliga invändning mot att behålla båda kanalerna är dubbel administration, inte att telefonanvändare är lata eller dyra.” Same institutional subject and same motive exclusion. “Costly” renders “dyra” in the supplied characterisation of users; it does not deny the possibility of labour costs associated with a channel. Supported.\\n\\n23. **“Yet the papers contain neither a measurement of staff time nor a calculation of financial savings.”** Source: “Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna.” Same document-scoped absence. “Financial savings” preserves the economic object of the calculation. Supported.\\n\\n24. **“Before making a permanent change, the board should be able to weigh the labour cost against the value for users.”** Source: “Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.” Also: “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.” The proposed timing and comparison are preserved as Sanna’s advocated decision standard. Supported.\\n\\n25. **“The pilot’s booking totals cannot settle that judgement.”** Source: “Rapporten räknar bokningar, inte unika personer. Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana.” Also: “Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna.” The judgement is the immediately preceding labour-cost/user-value comparison. The author reasons from the defined limits of booking counts and the absent cost measurement. She does not claim that the report is devoid of evidence of every kind. Supported argument.\\n\\n26. **Heading: “A wider trial can inform the decision”.** Source: “Öppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar. Under försöket ska förvaltningen registrera tidsåtgång per bokningsväg och be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb.” “Wider” has an explicit comparator: two pilot premises versus all seven. “Can inform” is the advocated trial’s prospective use, not a claim that a causal effect has already been demonstrated. Supported argument.\\n\\n27. **“Our proposal covers all seven venues for six months, keeping both booking routes.”** Source: “Öppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar.” Organisation, seven-premises coverage, six-month duration and retention of both routes are preserved. “Our” is the spokesperson’s institutional voice. Supported.\\n\\n28. **“Staff would record the time spent on each route”.** Source: “Under försöket ska förvaltningen registrera tidsåtgång per bokningsväg”. Administration acting through staff is compatible with the specified recording work and the source’s earlier “personalen”. The draft’s conditional presents what the proposed trial would entail, preserving the fact that it has not been adopted. Same unit: time by route. Supported.\\n\\n29. **“and ask users to explain their choice voluntarily.”** Source: “Under försöket ska förvaltningen registrera tidsåtgång per bokningsväg och be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb.” The scope “under försöket” continues from the proposed trial. The choice is between the same two stated routes. Voluntariness modifies the users’ explanation, not the staff’s recording duty. Supported.\\n\\n30. **“This would help the board assess both the workload and users’ reasons.”** Source: “Under försöket ska förvaltningen registrera tidsåtgång per bokningsväg och be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb.” Also: “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.” This is the author’s stated purpose for collecting precisely those records and reasons. It claims prospective informational help, without promising a particular outcome or asserting a measured causal effect. Supported argument.\\n\\n31. **Heading: “The board should postpone the permanent switch”.** Source: “Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.” The actor, permanence and requested decision are preserved. Supported advocacy.\\n\\n32. **“Öppna beslut makes no claim to have funded or costed the trial.”** Source: “Föreningen gör inget anspråk på att ha finansierat eller kostnadsberäknat försöket.” This accurately reports the limits of the organisation’s claims. It does not assert that funding or costing never occurred. Supported.\\n\\n33. **“The executive board must consider its cost”.** Source: “Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till.” This retains cost as an unresolved matter requiring the board’s consideration. “Its” refers to the proposed trial, as in the immediately preceding source sentence. No amount, funding source or affordability assessment is invented. Supported.\\n\\n34. **“postpone the permanent switch and first test both routes.”** Source: “Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.” The modal “must” is the author’s advocated obligation, not a purported statutory requirement. The source expressly permits a sharp position and forbids invented legal requirements; this passage states the position. Supported.\\n\\n35. **“It can then decide whether a booking channel should be removed, changed or retained.”** Source: “Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till. Därefter kan den avgöra om en kanal ska tas bort, ändras eller behållas.” Read with the brief’s “först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb”, the draft’s “then” comes after considering the trial cost and testing both routes. All three possible channel decisions remain available. The draft does not promise that telephone will be permanently retained or that a channel must be removed. Supported.\\n\\n## Pronoun and related-reference accounting\\n\\nReferent identification and person/number/gender are assessed separately below. English institutional “it/its” marks no natural gender. Swedish common/neuter grammatical gender is not treated as evidence of a human’s sex or gender.\\n\\n| Draft form | Referent and source | Person and number conveyed | Natural-gender information and support |\\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\\n| “each route”, “why people use it” | The singular distributive item is one of telephone/web; “användarna … telefon eller webb” and “per bokningsväg”. “People” is plural human users. | “it”: third person singular; “people”: plural. | No natural gender asserted. Swedish “den” agrees with grammatical common gender in “kommunstyrelsen”; it does not identify a woman. |\\n\\nNo unsupported pronoun person, number or natural-gender assertion was found.\\n\\n## Source-support findings and bidirectional cases\\n\\n### F1 — The absent digital variable changes\\n\\n**Draft:** “It did not measure age, functional ability or familiarity with digital technology.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana.”\\n\\n**Difference:** The draft turns a statement about absent measurement of digital experience/practice into a statement about absent measurement of familiarity with digital technology. A missing population denominator can prevent a proportion without preventing an absolute count. The source also names inability to book digitally; the draft names inability to book online. The web is the discussed digital route, but using the source’s own term avoids expanding this separate population assertion.\\n\\n**Source true, draft false:** In a compatible situation, the 24 telephone bookings are known through a separate one-booking-per-participant trial protocol to belong to 24 different residents who cannot complete the web booking process. The report itself still counts bookings, rather than reporting a unique-person count; the protocol and direct knowledge of those participants’ booking ability are not measurements of age, functional ability or digital experience. There is no translated-quotation finding.\\n\\n## Completion status\\n\\nComplete: the whole draft, including title, standfirst, byline, headings, factual implications and pronoun features, has been compared with the full supplied brief and source. The source supports this limitation both with “Rapporten räknar bokningar, inte unika personer” and with Sanna’s “Att en bokning går via telefon är inte ett bevis för att någon inte kan använda webben.” The report’s counterexample explicitly adds a “separate one-booking-per-participant trial protocol” and “separately established inability”, and produces an “absolute number for these residents”, a trial subset, rather than the number of residents unable to book online. Those additions do not make the draft’s actual assertion about the supplied figures false. No prose change is made.\\n\\nMinor report wording qualification: item 8 describes “acceptance of digital booking”. The exact draft says “I do not oppose digital booking” and the source says “Hon motsätter sig inte digital bokning.” They establish lack of opposition rather than positive endorsement. Its actual pairing is sound at that narrower proposition. No prose repair is required.\\n\\nThe final delivery account will identify F1, the differing source variable and the checker’s proposed repair, state that the compared draft is delivered with a known defect, and explain the skill’s prohibition on changing prose after the final comparison. No blanket fidelity claim will be made. F2’s rejection rests on the exact contextual evidence above, not on checker approval.\\n\\nLifecycle status: the native Harness confirms both run-owned checker tasks are completed. No outstanding run-owned wait helper exists. The compared draft will be preserved verbatim in the response with only the resolved Kntnt frontmatter added. The internal reports and scratch copies may be removed after this validation is recorded; all needed unresolved-finding accounting is retained for the response.\\n\";\ntext(await tools.exec_command({cmd:\"python3 - <<'PY'\\nimport json\\nimport pathlib\\nimport shutil\\nrun_dir = pathlib.Path(\"+JSON.stringify(state.run_dir)+\")\\nexpected_draft = \"+JSON.stringify(draft)+\"\\nif (run_dir / 'draft.md').read_text(encoding='utf-8') != expected_draft:\\n    raise RuntimeError('Scratch draft differs from the compared prose retained for response; cleanup stopped.')\\n(run_dir / 'validation-2.md').write_text(\"+JSON.stringify(disposition)+\", encoding='utf-8')\\nshutil.rmtree(run_dir)\\nscratch = run_dir.parent\\nbaseline = \"+JSON.stringify(state.scratch_baseline)+\"\\nremaining = sorted(p.name for p in scratch.iterdir())\\nrun_owned_leftovers = [p.name for p in scratch.iterdir() if (p.name.startswith('write-uv-') or p.name == run_dir.name) and p.name not in baseline]\\nprint(json.dumps({'draft_preserved_for_response': True, 'source_check_scratch_removed': not run_dir.exists(), 'scratch_matches_baseline': remaining == baseline, 'run_owned_leftovers': run_owned_leftovers}))\\nPY\",max_output_tokens:2000}));\nstore(\"write_artifact\",\"---\\nkntnt:\\n  genre: opinion\\n  technique: none\\n  language: en_GB\\n---\\n\\n\"+draft);\ntext(load(\"write_artifact\"));\n", "internal_chat_message_metadata_passthrough": {"turn_id": "01a0f746-c0d6-7bd0-8143-630450bece1f", "create_time": 1790856430.016756}}}
{"session": "01a0f746-bf0e-7cb3-80cd-9c63c2d7bef2", "timestamp": "2026-10-01T12:07:44.880Z", "result": {"type": "custom_tool_call_output", "id": "ctco_01a0f75d-09f0-73d3-92b8-f822223bba1f", "call_id": "call_SeKXq70wAN7gTmwcC4Pzl09v", "output": [{"type": "input_text", "text": "Script completed\nWall time 0.2 seconds\nOutput:\n"}, {"type": "input_text", "text": "{\"chunk_id\":\"34a1cf\",\"wall_time_seconds\":0.000003916,\"exit_code\":0,\"original_token_count\":35,\"output\":\"{\\\"draft_preserved_for_response\\\": true, \\\"source_check_scratch_removed\\\": true, \\\"scratch_matches_baseline\\\": true, \\\"run_owned_leftovers\\\": []}\\n\"}"}, {"type": "input_text", "text": "---\nkntnt:\n  genre: opinion\n  technique: none\n  language: en_GB\n---\n\n# Lervik needs evidence before ending telephone bookings\n\nThe case for removing telephone bookings from Lervik’s association premises rests on avoiding duplicate administration. But the papers provide no measurement of staff time or calculated savings. The municipal executive board should establish what each route costs and why people use it.\n\nBy Sanna Ek, spokesperson for Öppna beslut\n\nA staff report for the 18 June meeting of Lervik’s municipal executive board proposes ending telephone booking at all seven association premises from September. Öppna beslut wants that permanent switch postponed while both telephone and web booking are tested for six months. I do not oppose digital booking; I question whether the evidence justifies removing the alternative.\n\n## The pilot figures leave users’ reasons unanswered\n\nThe municipality’s pilot report, Booking Association Premises, dated 8 April 2026, records 96 web bookings and 24 telephone bookings during an eight-week trial at two venues. Those figures describe bookings, not unique individuals.\n\nIt did not measure age, functional ability or familiarity with digital technology. We cannot use the telephone bookings to calculate how many residents cannot book online. A booking made by telephone proves no inability to use the web; it gives us a reason to ask why that route is still used.\n\n## Duplicate administration deserves a fair assessment\n\nThe administration wants staff to avoid entering information through two workflows. That objection deserves to be taken seriously. Its case concerns duplicate administration, not a claim that telephone users are lazy or costly.\n\nYet the papers contain neither a measurement of staff time nor a calculation of financial savings. Before making a permanent change, the board should be able to weigh the labour cost against the value for users. The pilot’s booking totals cannot settle that judgement.\n\n## A wider trial can inform the decision\n\nOur proposal covers all seven venues for six months, keeping both booking routes.\n\nStaff would record the time spent on each route and ask users to explain their choice voluntarily. This would help the board assess both the workload and users’ reasons.\n\n## The board should postpone the permanent switch\n\nÖppna beslut makes no claim to have funded or costed the trial. The executive board must consider its cost, postpone the permanent switch and first test both routes. It can then decide whether a booking channel should be removed, changed or retained.\n"}], "internal_chat_message_metadata_passthrough": {"turn_id": "01a0f746-c0d6-7bd0-8143-630450bece1f", "create_time": 1790856464.880152}}}
